n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})/2\leq\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil
then both sides of (
S15
) are zero by the definition of
\tilde{k}_{\omega}
. If
k_{\omega}\geq n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})/2>\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil
, then the right hand side of (
S15
) is greater than
2C_{\mathrm{B}}
, since
C_{S}\geq 1
, so the result follows from the bound on
f_{\omega^{\prime}}
given in Proposition
S4
. If
\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil\leq k_{\omega}<n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})/2
, then
\tilde{k}_{\omega}=k_{\omega}
, and from the definition of
E_{1}^{\delta}
and the smoothness of
f_{\omega^{\prime}}
(see (
10
)
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\dots,k_{\omega}\}}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))\!-\!f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{S}}\max_{i\in\{1,\dots,k_{\omega}\}}\|X_{(i)_{\omega}}^{\omega}\!-\!x^{\omega}\|^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2k_{\omega}}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\right)^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega}}\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2k_{\omega}}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}.

Finally, if
k_{\omega}<\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil<n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})/2
, then similarly we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in\{1,\dots,\tilde{k}_{\omega}\}}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\right|

\displaystyle=\max_{i\in\{1,\dots,\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil\}}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\right)^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}.

The result follows since
C_{\mathrm{B}}\geq 1
.
∎

First note that for
\omega=\mathbf{0}_{d}
and for
x\in\mathcal{X}
, on the event
B_{\mathbf{0}_{d}}^{\delta}(x)
we have that

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\mathbf{0}_{d}}(x)-f_{\mathbf{0}_{d}}(x)\bigr|=\Bigl|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}-\mathbb{P}_{P}(Y=1)\Bigr|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n}}=R_{\mathbf{0}_{d},2}.

Thus the result holds for
\omega=\mathbf{0}_{d}
.

Recall from Algorithm
1

that for
\omega\in\mathcal{N}\setminus\{\mathbf{0}_{d}\}
we have

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)=\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(x)-\frac{1}{2}-\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x).

(S16)

Then using also the fact that
\eta_{\omega}(x)=1/2+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)
, we have that

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)\!-\!f_{\omega}(x)\bigr|

\displaystyle=\Bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)\!-\!\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))+\frac{1}{2}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))\!-\!f_{\omega}(x)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\leq\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-f_{\omega}(x)\Bigr|+\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(x)-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))\bigr\}\Bigr|\\
&\hskip 60.0pt+\Bigl|\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\Bigl\{\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\Bigr\}\Bigr|.\end{split}

(S17)

On the event
E_{1}^{\delta}(x)
the first term in (
S17
) is bounded by
R_{\omega,1}(x)
by Lemma
S27
, and on the event
E_{2}^{\delta}(x)
the second term in (
S17
) is bounded by
R_{\omega,2}
. For the third term, on
E_{1}^{\delta}(x)\cap E_{2}^{\delta}(x)
, first by Lemma
S27
we have

\displaystyle\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(x))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\Bigr|+\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq R_{\omega,1}(x)+\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\bigr|,

for
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
. Suppose now for induction, that (
18
)
holds for all
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
. Then using the facts that
R_{\omega^{\prime},1}(x)\leq R_{\omega,1}(x)
and
R_{\omega^{\prime},2}\leq R_{\omega,2}
for all
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)-f_{\omega}(x)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2^{d_{\omega}}\cdot R_{\omega,1}(x)+R_{\omega,2}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq(1+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime}})\cdot\Bigl(2^{d_{\omega}}\cdot R_{\omega,1}(x)+R_{\omega,2}\Bigr).

The constant
\kappa_{\omega}
can then be calculated using
\kappa_{\textbf{0}}=1
and the relation
\kappa_{\omega}:=1+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime}}
, for
d_{\omega}>1
. As in Proposition
S4
, this sequence is related to the ordered Bell numbers via
\kappa_{\omega}=2B_{d_{\omega}}:=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{j^{d_{\omega}}}{2^{j}}\leq 2(d_{\omega}+1)^{d_{\omega}}
, for
d_{\omega}>1

(
Zou, 2018
, see e.g.)
.
∎

First let
\epsilon:=C\cdot R_{\Omega}^{\delta}(x)
, so that
x\in\mathcal{X}
satisfies
|\eta(x)-1/2|\geq\epsilon
. We claim that on the event
E_{1}^{\delta}(x)\cap E_{2}^{\delta}(x)
, we have

|\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)-f_{\omega}(x)|\leq\frac{\epsilon}{2|\Omega\cup L(\Omega)|}

(S18)

for all
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
. Then, if
\eta(x)-1/2\geq\epsilon
, using that
\eta(x)=1/2+\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})}f_{\omega}(x)=1/2+\sum_{\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)}f_{\omega}(x)
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\eta}_{\Omega}(x)-1/2=\sum_{\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)}\bigl\{\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)-f_{\omega}(x)\bigr\}+\eta(x)-1/2\geq-\epsilon/2+\epsilon>0.

By symmetry, if
1/2-\eta(x)\geq\epsilon
, we have that
1/2-\hat{\eta}_{\Omega}(x)\geq\epsilon/2>0
.

It remains that to show that (
S18
) holds. First note that since
\eta(x)\in[0,1]
, we have that
\epsilon\leq 1/2
.
Thus, for
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
, we have that

\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\rceil\leq 4\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)\leq\frac{n_{\omega}^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}{C^{2}}\leq k_{\omega}.

Moreover, for
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
,

\frac{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}{2}\geq 2^{d_{\omega}/\beta_{\omega}-1}C^{d_{\omega}/\beta_{\omega}}n_{\omega}^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\geq 1+\lfloor n_{\omega}^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\rfloor=k_{\omega},

since
C>2
. Therefore
\tilde{k}_{\omega}=k_{\omega}
, for
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
.
It follows that

\Bigl(\frac{2\tilde{k}_{\omega}}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\Bigr)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}=\Bigl(\frac{2k_{\omega}}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\Bigr)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}\leq\Bigl(\frac{4n_{\omega}^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}{n_{\omega}\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})}\Bigr)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}\leq\frac{4n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}^{2}/d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}{\rho_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}(x^{\omega})}.

Then, recalling the definitions of
R_{\omega,1}(x)
and
R_{\omega,2}
from (
17
)
, we have

\displaystyle R_{\omega,1}(x)

\displaystyle=2C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\max_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\Bigl(\frac{2\tilde{k}_{\omega^{\prime}}}{n_{\omega^{\prime}}\rho_{\omega^{\prime}}(x^{\omega^{\prime}})}\Bigr)^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega^{\prime}}}\leq 8C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot R_{\Omega}^{\delta}(x),

(S19)

for
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
.
Further

R_{\omega,2}=\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2k_{\omega}}}\leq\frac{\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2^{1/2}n_{\omega}^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}\leq\frac{1}{2^{1/2}}R_{\omega}^{\delta}(x)\leq\frac{1}{2^{1/2}}R_{\Omega}^{\delta}(x).

(S20)

Finally, by Lemma
3

, along with (
S19
) and (
S20
), for
\omega\in\Omega\cup L(\Omega)
, we have that

\displaystyle|\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)-f_{\omega}(x)|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{\omega}\cdot(2^{d_{\omega}}R_{\omega,1}(x)+R_{\omega,2})

\displaystyle\leq\kappa_{\omega}\cdot\Bigl(2^{3+d_{\omega}}C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}+\frac{1}{2^{1/2}}\Bigr)\cdot R^{\delta}(x)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{2|\Omega\cup L(\Omega)|}\cdot R^{\delta}(x)=\frac{\epsilon}{2|\Omega\cup L(\Omega)|},

where the last equality holds since
C\geq 2\kappa_{\omega}|\Omega\cup L(\Omega)|\bigl(2^{3+d_{\omega}}C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}+\sqrt{1/2}\bigr)
. This establishes the claim in (
S18
) and completes the proof.
∎

Our events
E_{1}^{\delta}(x)
and
E_{2}^{\delta}(x)
give us control of the properties of our estimators at each fixed point
x\in\mathcal{X}
. Ideally we’d have this control simultaneously for all
x\in\mathcal{X}
, but this will typically not be possible. Instead, we only require this control at a large portion of the feature domain in order to control the excess risk. We then show that the remaining portion of the feature domain is relatively small with high probability. More precisely, for
\delta\in(0,1)
,
\Omega\subseteq\{0,1\}^{d}
and
\alpha\in[0,\infty)
, let
\delta^{\prime}:=\frac{\delta}{2}\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha}\}
,
and define

\mathcal{X}_{\delta}=\mathcal{X}_{\delta}(D_{n}):=\Bigl\{x\in\mathcal{X}:E_{1}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)\cap E_{2}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)\ \text{holds}\Bigr\}.

Lemma S28
.

We have
\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Bigl\{\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}(1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha})\Bigr\}\leq\delta
.

By Markov’s inequality and Lemmas
S25
and
S26
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\Bigl\{\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}(1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha})\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha}\}}\cdot\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\{\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha}\}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\Bigl(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c}\}}\,d\mu(x)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{1/n_{\omega}^{1+\alpha}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Bigl\{\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}(E_{1}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)^{c})+\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}(E_{2}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)^{c})\Bigr\}\,d\mu(x)

\displaystyle\leq\delta.

∎

We are now in a position to provide the proofs of Proposition
5

and subsequently the upper bound in Theorem
1

.

Recall the constant
C
from the statement of Lemma
4

. For
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
, let

\lambda_{\omega}:=\Bigl(\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\Bigr)^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}^{2}/d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}},\quad\text{and}\quad t_{\omega}:=\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{\alpha d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}d_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}

Define the partition
(\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j})_{j=0}^{\infty}
of
\mathcal{X}_{\delta}
given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{\delta,0}

\displaystyle:=\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta}:~t_{\omega}\leq\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega}),\ \text{for all}\ \omega\in\Omega\}

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}

\displaystyle:=\Bigl\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta}:~2^{-j}t_{\omega}\leq\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega}),\ \text{for all}\ \omega\in\Omega\Bigr\}\setminus\bigcup_{j^{\prime}\in[j-1]\cup\{0\}}\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j^{\prime}}.

Then, by Lemma
4

, for any
j\in\mathbb{N}\cup\{0\}
and any
x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}
, we have that

\displaystyle|2\eta(x)-1|\cdot

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}}

\displaystyle<2C\cdot R^{\delta^{\prime}}_{\Omega}(x)\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}\}}

\displaystyle=2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\biggl(\frac{n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\alpha\beta_{\omega}^{2}/d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}{\rho_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}(x^{\omega})}+\log^{1/2}_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\biggr)n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\biggl\}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}\}}

\displaystyle\leq 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\biggl(2^{j\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}+\log^{1/2}_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\cdot\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggl\}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}\}},

since
\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)=C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)
on the event
E_{1}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)\cap E_{2}^{\delta^{\prime}}(x)
. Now, looking at the separate elements of the partition in turn, first since
P\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{M}}(\alpha,C_{\mathrm{M}})
, we have that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}

\displaystyle|2\eta(x)-1|\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,0}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}}\,d\mu(x)

\displaystyle\leq\mu\bigl(\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,0}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}\bigr)\cdot 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(1+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\biggl(\biggl\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:|\eta(x)-1/2|<C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\Bigl\{\Bigl(1+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\Bigr\}\biggr\}\biggr)

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\cdot 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(1+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\mathrm{M}}C^{1+\alpha}\Bigl(1+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)^{1+\alpha}\cdot\Bigl(\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{1+\alpha}.

(S21)

Further, for
j\geq 1
, since
Q\in\mathcal{Q}_{\mathrm{L}}(\bm{\gamma},C_{\mathrm{L}},\mathcal{O})
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|2\eta(x)-1|\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}}\,d\mu(x)

\displaystyle\leq\mu\bigl(\mathcal{X}_{\delta,j}\bigr)\cdot 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(2^{j\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle=\mu\Bigl(\bigcup_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})<2^{-(j-1)}t_{\omega}\}\Bigr)\cdot 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(2^{j\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\omega\in\Omega}\mu_{\omega}\bigl(\{\rho_{\omega}(x^{\omega})<2^{-(j-1)}t_{\omega}\}\bigr)\cdot 2C\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(2^{j\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2CC_{\mathrm{L}}|\Omega|\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{(2^{-(j-1)}t_{\omega})^{\gamma_{\omega}}\}\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\biggl\{\Bigl(2^{j\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\frac{\lambda_{\omega}}{t_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq 2CC_{\mathrm{L}}|\Omega|2^{-(j-1)\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}}\cdot\Bigl(2^{j\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}\!+\!\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)\cdot\Bigl(\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{1+\alpha}.

(S22)

Here we have used that
t_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}=\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}\alpha}{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}
. Observe further that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}2^{-j\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}}\cdot\Bigl(2^{j\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}+\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\Bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt=\frac{1}{2^{\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}-1}+\frac{\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})}{2^{\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}}-1}

\displaystyle\hskip 120.0pt\leq\frac{2\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})}{2^{\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}-\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}-1}

(S23)

since
\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}>\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}
.
It follows from (
S3.1
), (
S3.1
) and (
S3.1
) that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\Omega})

\displaystyle=\int_{\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}}|2\eta(x)-1|\,d\mu(x)

\displaystyle=\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|2\eta(x)-1|\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{x\in\mathcal{X}_{j}:\hat{C}_{\Omega}(x)\neq C^{\mathrm{Bayes}}(x)\}}\,d\mu(x)

\displaystyle\leq\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})

\displaystyle\hskip-38.0pt\!+\!\Bigl\{2C_{\mathrm{M}}C^{1+\alpha}\log_{+}^{(1+\alpha)/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})\!+\!\frac{4CC_{\mathrm{L}}|\Omega|\cdot\log_{+}^{1/2}(2|\mathcal{N}|/\delta^{\prime})}{2^{-\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}-2^{-\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}}}\Bigr\}\Bigl(\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{1+\alpha}.

Finally observe that

\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{d_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}=n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\geq n_{\omega}^{-(1+\alpha)}.

Thus, by Lemma
S28
, we have

\mathbb{P}\Bigl(\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\lambda_{\omega}^{\frac{\gamma_{\omega}d_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}d_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)\leq\mathbb{P}\Bigl(\mu(\mathcal{X}_{\delta}^{c})\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-(1+\alpha)}\Bigr)\leq\delta.

The conclusion then follows with

K_{\Omega}:=1+2C_{\mathrm{M}}C^{1+\alpha}+\frac{4CC_{\mathrm{L}}|\Omega|}{2^{-\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}\}}-2^{-\min_{\omega\in\Omega}\gamma_{\omega}}}.

∎

Fix
\Omega\in\mathcal{I}(\{0,1\}^{d})
. Let

\delta_{0}:=\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}\{n_{\omega}^{-(1+\alpha)}\}\leq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{3\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}

and let

t_{0}:=K_{\Omega}\cdot\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(\frac{2|\mathcal{N}|}{\delta_{0}}\Bigr)\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}

Note also that
\delta_{0}\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-3(1+\alpha)/2}
, and thus

\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(\frac{2|\mathcal{N}|}{\delta_{0}}\Bigr)\leq(3(1+\alpha)/2)^{(1+\alpha)/2}\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(2|\mathcal{N}|\min_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}\Bigr).

By Proposition
5

, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}

\displaystyle\bigl\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\Omega})\bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}+\mathbb{P}_{Q}\bigl\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\Omega})>t_{0}\mid O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\bigr\}

\displaystyle\leq K_{\Omega}\cdot\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(\frac{2|\mathcal{N}|}{\delta_{0}}\Bigr)\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}+\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl\{1+2K_{\Omega}\cdot\Bigl(\frac{3(1+\alpha)}{2}\Bigr)^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}(2^{d+1})\Bigr\}\cdot\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(\min_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}\Bigr)\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}.

The upper bound in Theorem
1

follows by taking
\Omega=\Omega_{\star}
if
\Omega_{\star}\subseteq\mathcal{N}
, and otherwise using the trivial bound that
\mathbb{E}_{Q}\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\Omega})\mid O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\}\leq 1
.
∎

S3.2
Proof of Theorem
2

The proof of Theorem
2

involves controlling the additional error that we incur from choosing which of the
\hat{f}_{\omega}
to set to zero based on the training data. Recall the definitions of
\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}
,
\tau_{\omega}
and the set
\hat{\Omega}\in\mathcal{I}(\{0,1\}^{d})
from Algorithm
1

. As in Sections
5.2

,
5.3

and
S3.1
, we treat the missingness indications
o_{1},\ldots,o_{n}
as fixed and all probability statements in this section should also be interpreted as being conditional on
O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}
.

To prove the key Proposition
6

in Section
5.2

, we introduce four high-probability events. The first event,
E_{3}^{\delta}
, gives us control of the average signal at the training points for every observation pattern
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
, i.e. it ensures that
1/n_{\omega}\cdot\sum f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})
is sufficiently large, and the second event,
E_{4}^{\delta}
, bounds the number of training data points falling into the tail of the marginal distributions. The third and fourth events,
E_{5}^{\delta}
and
E_{6}^{\delta}
, respectively, are similar to the events
E_{1}^{\delta}(x)
and
E_{2}^{\delta}(x)
from the Subsection
5.2

, and allow us to estimate the error between
\hat{f}_{\omega}
and
f_{\omega}
at each of the training points in the body of the distribution, that is, those which are not in the tails. Lemmas
S29
to
S32
bound the probability of the complement of
E_{\circ}^{\delta}:=E_{3}^{\delta}\cap E_{4}^{\delta}\cap E_{5}^{\delta}\cap E_{6}^{\delta}
. Then, working on this event, Lemmas
S33
and
S34
provide the rate at which we control
|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-f_{\omega}(X_{j})|
uniformly over all training points
X_{j}
s in the body of the distribution. Finally, the conclusion of these results is that
\hat{\Omega}=\Omega_{\star}
on the event
E_{\circ}^{\delta}
, which proves Proposition
6

.

Recall that
N_{\omega}=\{i\in[n]:\omega\preceq o_{i}\}
, then for
\omega\in\mathcal{N}\cap\Omega_{\star}
let

\displaystyle D^{\delta}_{\omega}:=\biggl\{\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})>\sigma_{\omega}^{2}-C^{2}_{\mathrm{B}}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}\biggr\},

and let
E_{3}^{\delta}:=\bigcap_{\omega\in\mathcal{N}\cap\Omega_{\star}}D_{\omega}^{\delta}
.

Lemma S29
.

For
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have
\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\bigl((E_{3}^{\delta})^{c}\bigr)\leq\delta.

Fix
\omega\in\mathcal{N}\cap\Omega_{\star}
, then
\{f^{2}_{\omega}(X_{i})\}_{i\in N_{\omega}}
are independent random variables with mean
\mathbb{E}_{Q}\{f_{\omega}^{2}(X)|O=o_{i}\}\geq\sigma_{\omega}^{2}
, which take values in
[0,C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}]
by Proposition
S4
. Then by Hoeffding’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Biggl(\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})-\sigma_{\omega}^{2}\leq-C^{2}_{\mathrm{B}}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}\Biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Biggl(\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})-\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbb{E}_{Q}\{f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})\mid O_{i}=o_{i}\}\leq-C^{2}_{\mathrm{B}}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}\Biggr)\leq\frac{\delta}{|\mathcal{N}|}.

Taking a union bound gives the desired result.
∎

We will also require control on the number of training data points that lie in the tail of the marginal feature distribution. As in the previous subsection, for
\omega\in\{0,1\}^{d}
, we write
\rho_{\omega}(x)
in place of
\min_{o\in\mathcal{O}:o\succeq\omega}\rho_{\mu_{\omega|o},d_{\omega}}(x)
. Then, for
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
and
r_{\omega}\in(0,1)
, let

\displaystyle F^{\delta}_{\omega}=F^{\delta}_{\omega}(r_{\omega}):=\biggl\{\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{i}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}\}}<2C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}\biggr\},

and let
E_{4}^{\delta}:=\bigcap_{\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}}F_{\omega}^{\delta}
.

Lemma S30
.

Fix
\mathcal{O}\subseteq\{0,1\}^{d}
,
\delta\in(0,1)
,
\bm{\gamma}\in[0,\infty)^{d}
,
C_{\mathrm{L}}\geq 1
and
Q\in\mathcal{Q}^{+}_{\mathrm{L}}(\bm{\gamma},C_{\mathrm{L}},\mathcal{O})
. Then if
r_{\omega}\geq\Bigl(\frac{4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{C_{\mathrm{L}}n_{\omega}}\Bigr)^{1/\gamma_{\omega}}
for all
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, we have
\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\bigl((E_{4}^{\delta})^{c}\bigr)\leq\delta.

Fix
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
, then for
i\in N_{\omega}
, we have that
\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{i}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}\}}
are independent Bernoulli random variables with mean
\mu_{\omega|o_{i}}(\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}:\rho_{\omega}(x)<r_{\omega}\})\leq C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}
, since
Q\in\mathcal{Q}^{+}_{\mathrm{L}}(\bm{\gamma},C_{\mathrm{L}},\mathcal{O})
. Then by a multiplicative Chernoff bound
(
Billingsley, 1995
, Theorem 9.3)
, we have

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\biggl(\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{i}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}\}}\geq 2C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}\biggr)\leq\exp(-n_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}/4)\leq\frac{\delta}{|\mathcal{N}|},

since
r_{\omega}>\bigl(\frac{4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{C_{\mathrm{L}}n_{\omega}}\bigr)^{1/\gamma_{\omega}}
. Taking a union bound over
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
gives the desired result.
∎

Our final two events provide control of the variance and bias of our estimates of
f_{\omega}
, for
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
, evaluated at the appropriate training data points. For
\delta\in(0,1)
and
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
let

\displaystyle G^{\delta}_{\omega}:=\bigcap_{j\in N_{\omega}}\Biggl\{\biggl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\Bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\Bigr\}\biggr|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega}}}\Biggr\},

and let
E_{5}^{\delta}:=\bigcap_{\omega\in\mathcal{N}}G_{\omega}^{\delta}
. Moreover, for any
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
,
\delta\in(0,1)
,
n_{\omega}\geq 2
, and
r_{\omega}\in(0,1)
, let

\displaystyle H_{\omega}^{\delta}

\displaystyle:=\bigcap_{j\in N_{\omega}}\!\biggl\{\lVert X_{({k}_{\omega})_{\omega}}^{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\!-\!X_{j}^{\omega}\rVert\!\cdot\!\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}\}}\leq\Bigl(\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\Bigr)^{\frac{1}{d_{\omega}}}\biggr\}

and let
E_{6}^{\delta}:=\bigcap_{\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}}H_{\omega}^{\delta}
.

Lemma S31
.

For
\delta\in(0,1)
, we have
\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\bigl((E_{5}^{\delta})^{c}\bigr)\leq\delta.

Fix
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
and
j\in N_{\omega}
. The labels
Y_{(1)_{\omega}}(X_{j}),\ldots,Y_{(n_{\omega})_{\omega}}(X_{j})
are conditionally independent given
(X_{\ell}^{\omega})_{\ell\in N_{\omega}}
. Further, for
i\in[n_{\omega}]
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})\big|(X_{\ell}^{\omega})_{\ell\in N_{\omega}}\bigr\}=\mathbb{E}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})\big|X_{(i)_{\omega}}^{\omega}(X_{j})\bigr\}=\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})).

Then by Hoeffding’s inequality, we have that

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Biggl(\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\bigr\}\Bigr|>\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega}}}\Biggm|(X_{\ell}^{\omega})_{\ell\in N_{\omega}}\Biggr)\leq\frac{\delta}{|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}}.

Taking expectation over
(X_{\ell}^{\omega})_{\ell\in N_{\omega}}
and using a union bound first over
j\in N_{\omega}
and then over
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
gives the result.
∎

Lemma S32
.

Fix
\delta\in(0,1)
and suppose that
n_{\omega}\geq 2
for every
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
. If
r_{\omega}>2(n_{\omega}-1)^{-1}\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}
for all
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, then
\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}((E_{6}^{\delta})^{c})\leq\delta
.

Fix
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
,
j\in N_{\omega}
and
x_{j}\in\mathcal{X}
. Then, if
\rho_{\omega}(x_{j}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}
, we have

\mathbb{P}\Biggl(\bigl\|X_{({k}_{\omega})_{\omega}}^{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\!-\!X_{j}^{\omega}\bigr\|\!\cdot\!\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}\}}>\biggl(\frac{2\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil\!\vee\!k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\biggr)^{\frac{1}{d_{\omega}}}\Biggm|X_{j}\!=\!x_{j}\Biggr)\!=\!0,

since the first term inside the probability is
0
.
On the other hand, if
\rho_{\omega}(x_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}
, then let

\displaystyle r:=\biggl(\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\biggr)^{1/d_{\omega}}<1,

where the inequality holds by the assumption on
r_{\omega}
. Therefore, by the definition of
\rho_{\omega}
, we have for
i\in N_{\omega}
,

\displaystyle\mu_{\omega|o_{i}}(B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega}))

\displaystyle\geq\rho_{\mu_{\omega|o_{i}},d_{\omega}}(x_{j}^{\omega})r^{d_{\omega}}\geq\rho_{\omega}(x_{j}^{\omega})r^{d_{\omega}}\geq r_{\omega}r^{d_{\omega}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)}\geq\frac{8\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{(n_{\omega}-1)}.

Then, using a multiplicative Chernoff bound, we obtain

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Bigl(\bigl\|X_{(k_{\omega})_{\omega}}^{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})-X_{j}^{\omega}\bigr\|

\displaystyle>r\Bigm|X_{j}=x_{j}\Bigr)\leq\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Bigl(\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{i}^{\omega}\in B_{r}(X_{j}^{\omega})\}}<k_{\omega}\Big|X_{j}=x_{j}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\Bigl(\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}\setminus\{j\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{i}^{\omega}\in B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega})\}}<k_{\omega}-1\Bigr)

\displaystyle\leq\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\biggl(\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}\setminus\{j\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{i}^{\omega}\in B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega})\}}<\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq\mbox{$\mathbb{P}$}\biggl(\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}\setminus\{j\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{X_{i}^{\omega}\in B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega})\}}<\frac{\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}\setminus\{j\}}\mu_{\omega|o_{i}}(B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega}))}{2}\biggr)

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}\setminus\{j\}}\mu_{\omega|o_{i}}(B_{r}(x_{j}^{\omega}))/8}\leq\frac{\delta}{|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}}.

Taking expectations over
X_{j}
, a union bound over
j\in N_{\omega}
, and another union bound over
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma S33
.

Fix
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
,
r_{\omega}\in(0,1)
,
\bm{\beta}\in(0,1]^{d}
, and
C_{\mathrm{S}}\geq 1
. Let
P\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{S}}(\bm{\beta},C_{\mathrm{S}})
and suppose that
n_{\omega}\geq 2
. Then on the event
E_{6}^{\delta}
, for any
\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega
, we have that

\displaystyle\max_{j\in N_{\omega}}\max_{i\in[k_{\omega}]}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\right|\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 90.0pt\leq 2C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\left(\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}.

(S24)

Fix
j\in N_{\omega}
. If
\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}
, then the left hand side of (
S33
) is
0
, so the result follows trivially. Suppose then for the remainder of the proof that
\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}
. If
(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}\leq 2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}
, then the right hand side is at least
2C_{\mathrm{B}}
, so the result follows from Proposition
S4
since
C_{S}\geq 1
. If
\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil\leq k_{\omega}<(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}/2
, then from the definition of
E_{6}^{\delta}
and the smoothness of
f_{\omega^{\prime}}
(since
P\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{S}}(\bm{\beta},C_{\mathrm{S}})
, see (
10
)
), we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[k_{\omega}]}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\!-\!f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{S}}\max_{i\in[k_{\omega}]}\|X_{(i)_{\omega}}^{\omega}(X_{j})\!-\!X_{j}^{\omega}\|^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2k_{\omega}}{(n_{\omega}\!-\!1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega}}\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2k_{\omega}}{(n_{\omega}\!-\!1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}.

Finally, if
k_{\omega}<\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil<(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}/2
, then similarly we have

\displaystyle\max_{i\in[k_{\omega}]}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{i\in[\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil]}\left|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{S}\left(\frac{2\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}.

The result follows since
C_{\mathrm{B}}\geq 1
.
∎

We are now in a position to control the bias and variance of the estimators
\hat{f}_{\omega}
, for
\omega\in\mathcal{N}
, evaluated at the feature observations in training data set. We first provide control of
\hat{f}_{\omega}
at every training point in the body of the distribution (i.e. those for which
\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}
) on the event
E_{5}^{\delta}\cap E_{6}^{\delta}
. Let
R_{\mathbf{0},\mathrm{T}}=\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n/\delta)}{2n}}
, and for
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}\setminus\{\mathbf{0}\}
, let

R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}:=\biggl[20C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\max_{0\prec\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\Bigl\{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)\cdot\Bigl(r_{\omega^{\prime}}(n_{\omega^{\prime}}\!-\!1)^{\frac{\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}(2\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}+d_{\omega^{\prime}})+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Bigr)^{-\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega^{\prime}}}\Bigr\}\biggr]\!\vee\!R_{\mathbf{0},\mathrm{T}}.

Lemma S34
.

Fix
\bm{\beta}\in(0,1]^{d}
,
C_{\mathrm{S}}\geq 1
, and suppose that
P\in\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{S}}(\bm{\beta},C_{\mathrm{S}})
. Suppose that
n_{\omega}\geq 2
and
r_{\omega}\geq 2(n_{\omega}-1)^{-1}\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}
for all
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, then on the event
E_{5}^{\delta}\cap E_{6}^{\delta}
, the following statements hold:

(i) For
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, we have that

\displaystyle\max_{j\in N_{\omega}}\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\bigr|\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}\}}\leq\kappa_{\omega,T}\cdot R_{\omega,T},

(S25)

where
1\leq\kappa_{\omega,T}\leq 2^{d_{\omega}(d_{\omega}+1)}
is given explicitly in the proof.

(ii) For
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
, we have that

\displaystyle\max_{j\in N_{\omega}}\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})\bigr|\leq\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},T}\cdot R_{\omega^{\prime},T}+\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},T}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega^{\prime}}}}.

First, for
\omega=\mathbf{0}_{d}
, on
E_{5}^{\delta}
we have that for
j\in[n]
that

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\mathbf{0}_{d}}(X_{j})-f_{\mathbf{0}_{d}}(X_{j})\bigr|=\Bigl|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}-\mathbb{P}_{P}(Y=1)\Bigr|\leq\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n/\delta)}{2n}}=R_{\mathbf{0},T}.

Thus the result holds for
\omega=\mathbf{0}_{d}
.

Now, for
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}\setminus\{\mathbf{0}_{d}\}
, fix
j\in N_{\omega}
. If
\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}
, then the result follows trivially, since the right hand side of (
S25
) is positive. We thus assume for the remainder of the proof that
\rho_{\omega}(X_{j}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}
. Now recall from Algorithm
1

that for
\omega\in\mathcal{N}\setminus\{\mathbf{0}_{d}\}
we have

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})=\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\frac{1}{2}-\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j}).

Then, since
\eta_{\omega}(X_{j})=1/2+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})
, we have that

\displaystyle\bigl|

\displaystyle\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\bigr|

\displaystyle=\Bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))+\frac{1}{2}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\Bigr|+\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\bigr\}\Bigr|

\displaystyle\hskip 60.0pt+\Bigl|\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\Bigl\{\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\Bigr\}\Bigr|.

(S26)

For the first term in (
S26
), by Lemma
S33
, on the event
E_{6}^{\delta}
we have

\displaystyle\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq 2C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\left(\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)r_{\omega}}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\frac{1}{r_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\cdot\left(\frac{\max\{5\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta),k_{\omega}\}}{(n_{\omega}-1)}\right)^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq 20C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\frac{1}{r_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\cdot\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{(n_{\omega}-1)^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}^{2}/d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}\leq R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}.

since
k_{\omega}\leq 4\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)(n_{\omega}-1)^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}
.
For the second term in (
S26
), on the event
E_{5}^{\delta}
we have

\displaystyle\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\bigr\}\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega}}}\leq\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{(n_{\omega}-1)^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}

\displaystyle\leq 20C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\cdot\frac{1}{r_{\omega}^{\beta_{\omega}/d_{\omega}}}\cdot\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{(n_{\omega}-1)^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}+\alpha\beta_{\omega}^{2}/d_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}}\leq R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}},

since
k_{\omega}\geq(n_{\omega}-1)^{\frac{2\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}
and
r_{\omega}\geq 2(n_{\omega}-1)^{-1}k_{\omega}
. For the third term in (
S26
),
similarly to above, by Lemma
S33
on
E_{6}^{\delta}
we have

\displaystyle\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))

\displaystyle-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\Bigr|+\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}+\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\bigr|,

for
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
, where we have used the lower bound on
r_{\omega}
. To finish the proof of part (i), suppose for induction that (
S25
) holds for all
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
. Then using the fact that
R_{\omega^{\prime},\mathrm{T}}\leq R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}
for all
\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-f_{\omega}(X_{j})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq(1+2^{d_{\omega}})R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\bigl|f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq(1+2^{d_{\omega}}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},\mathrm{T}})\cdot R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}.

The constant
\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}
can then be calculated using
\kappa_{\textbf{0},\mathrm{T}}=1
and the relation
\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}:=1+2^{d_{\omega}}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},\mathrm{T}}
, for
d_{\omega}>1
.

Now for part (ii), fix
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
. Then
f_{\omega}(X_{j})=0
and, on the event
E_{5}^{\delta}
, we have

\displaystyle\bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}

\displaystyle(X_{j})\bigr|=\Bigl|\hat{f}_{\omega}(X_{j})-\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))+\frac{1}{2}+\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\Bigr|

\displaystyle\hskip-8.0pt\leq\Bigl|\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}\bigl\{Y_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j})-\eta_{\omega}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))\bigr\}\Bigr|+\Bigl|\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\Bigl\{\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\Bigr\}\Bigr|.

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega}}}+\Bigl|\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\Bigl\{\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{i=1}^{k_{\omega}}f_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{(i)_{\omega}}(X_{j}))-\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(X_{j})\Bigr\}\Bigr|.

The proof is completed again via induction.
∎

Let
C_{\rho}:=(32C^{2}_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{L}})^{-\frac{1}{\min_{\omega\in\mathcal{N}}\gamma_{\omega}}}
, where
C_{\mathrm{B}}
is the constant from Proposition
S4
and recall
\phi_{\omega}=\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}
. Then, for
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, let
r_{\omega}:=C_{\rho}\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}}}
,
and consider the event
E_{\circ}^{\delta}=E^{\delta}_{3}\cap E^{\delta}_{4}\cap E^{\delta}_{5}\cap E^{\delta}_{6}
with these choices of
r_{\omega}
.
First note that, since

\displaystyle n_{\omega}

\displaystyle\geq\Bigl(\frac{32\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{C_{\rho}^{1\vee\gamma_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{2\gamma_{\omega}d_{\omega}+2\alpha\beta_{\omega}-\beta_{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\geq\max\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{C_{\rho}^{\gamma_{\omega}}C_{\mathrm{L}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}-\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}},\Bigl(\frac{32\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{C_{\rho}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}-\beta_{\omega}}},\Bigl(\frac{8}{C_{\rho}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}-4\gamma_{\omega}\beta_{\omega}-\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr\}

for
\omega\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}
, we have that

\displaystyle r_{\omega}\geq\max\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{C_{\mathrm{L}}n_{\omega}}\Bigr)^{1/\gamma_{\omega}},\frac{2\max\{\lceil 4\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\rceil,k_{\omega}\}}{n_{\omega}-1}\Bigr\}.

Therefore, by a union bound and Lemmas
S29
,
S30
,
S31
and
S32
, we have

\mathbb{P}_{Q}\bigl\{\bigl(E_{\circ}^{\delta}\bigr)^{c}\bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\bigr\}\leq 4\delta.

In the remainder of the proof we will show that, on the event
E_{\circ}^{\delta}
, we have
\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{\omega}\geq\tau_{\omega}
for every
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, and
\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}<\tau_{\omega}
for every
\omega\in\mathcal{N}\cap U(\Omega_{\star})
, and therefore that
\hat{\Omega}=\Omega_{\star}
. First consider
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
. On the event
E^{\delta}_{\circ}
, by Lemma
S34
and Proposition
S4
, we have that

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}^{2}_{\omega}-\sigma^{2}_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\hat{f}^{2}_{\omega}(X_{i})-\sigma^{2}_{\omega}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{i}^{\omega})\geq r_{\omega}\}}\{\hat{f}_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})-f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})\}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt+\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\rho_{\omega}(X_{i}^{\omega})<r_{\omega}\}}\{\hat{f}_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})-f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})\}+\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}f_{\omega}^{2}(X_{i})-\sigma_{\omega}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq-2C_{\mathrm{B}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\cdot R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}-8C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}-C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}.

Then, we have
\sigma_{\omega}^{2}\geq c_{\mathrm{E}}
since
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, and using the fact that

\tau_{\omega}=2^{-4}\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+2\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}=2^{-4}\cdot n_{\omega}^{-1/\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}
, we deduce that

\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}-\tau_{\omega}\geq c_{\mathrm{E}}-\tau_{\omega}-2C_{\mathrm{B}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\cdot R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}-8C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}}-C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}.

Therefore, we have
\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}\geq\tau_{\omega}
for all
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, if

\max\biggl\{2^{-4}\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+2\alpha\beta_{\omega}}},2C_{\mathrm{B}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}},8C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{L}}r_{\omega}^{\gamma_{\omega}},C_{\mathrm{B}}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{2n_{\omega}}}\biggr\}\leq c_{\mathrm{E}}/4

(S27)

for all
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
. To see that (
S27
) holds, first note that, for
\mathbf{0}_{d}\prec\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega
, we have

\displaystyle\Bigl(n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{2\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}(n_{\omega^{\prime}}-1)^{\frac{\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Bigr)^{-\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq\Bigl(n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{2\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}(n_{\omega^{\prime}}/2)^{\frac{\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Bigr)^{-\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}/d_{\omega^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}(\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}})}{\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\cdot n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}(2\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+2\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}-\beta_{\omega^{\prime}})}{2\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}}}

\displaystyle<2\cdot n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}}{2\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\leq 2\cdot n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}}}.

Moreover, since
n\geq n_{\omega^{\prime}}\geq n_{\omega}
, we have

\max\Bigl\{\sqrt{\frac{\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n/\delta)}{2n}},\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}}}\Bigr\}\leq\kappa_{\circ}\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}}},

for
\kappa_{\omega,\circ}=\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}
. It follows that

\displaystyle R_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}

\displaystyle\!=\!\biggl[20C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}\max_{0\prec\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\Bigl\{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)\!\cdot\!\Bigl(C_{\rho}\!\cdot\!n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{2\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}(n_{\omega^{\prime}}\!-\!1)^{\frac{\gamma_{\omega^{\prime}}d_{\omega^{\prime}}+\alpha\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{\phi_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Bigr)^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega^{\prime}}}{d_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Bigr\}\biggr]\!\vee\!R_{\mathbf{0},\mathrm{T}}

\displaystyle<40C_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{S}}C_{\rho}^{-\beta_{\omega}}\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}{2\phi_{\omega}}}.

Therefore, using that

\displaystyle n_{\omega}

\displaystyle\geq\Bigl(\frac{32C_{B}^{2}C_{\mathrm{L}}}{c_{\mathrm{E}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{\phi_{\omega}(2\vee\alpha^{-1})}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}}\Bigl(\frac{5C_{\mathrm{S}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{C_{\rho}^{\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}}

\displaystyle\geq\max\Bigl\{\Bigl(\frac{1}{4c_{\mathrm{E}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}},\Bigl(\frac{160\cdot C_{B}^{2}C_{\mathrm{S}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{C_{\rho}^{\beta_{\omega}}c_{\mathrm{E}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2\phi_{\omega}}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}},

\displaystyle\hskip 210.0pt\Bigl(\frac{32C^{2}_{\mathrm{B}}C_{\mathrm{L}}C_{\rho}^{\gamma_{\omega}}}{c_{\mathrm{E}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{\phi_{\omega}}{\alpha\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}},\frac{8\log_{+}(|\mathcal{N}|/\delta)}{c_{\mathrm{E}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{B}}^{4}}\Bigr\}

we deduce that (
S27
) holds. Note further that, since

n_{\omega}\geq\Bigl(\frac{320\cdot C_{B}C_{\mathrm{S}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{C_{\rho}^{\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{\frac{4\phi_{\omega}}{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}}},

we have
\kappa^{2}_{\omega,T}\cdot R^{2}_{\omega,T}\leq\tau_{\omega}/4
for all
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, which we will use in (
S28
) below.

For
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
, we have
f_{\omega}\equiv 0
. Thus, on the event
E^{\delta}_{4}\cap E^{\delta}_{5}\cap E^{\delta}_{6}
, by Lemma
S34
we have

\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{\omega}}\sum_{i\in N_{\omega}}\hat{f}^{2}_{\omega}(X_{i})

\displaystyle\leq\Biggl(\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in\{\Omega_{\star}\cup L(\Omega_{\star})\}\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},T}\cdot R_{\omega^{\prime},T}+\!\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},T}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)}{2k_{\omega^{\prime}}}}\Biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{\omega^{\prime}\in\Omega_{\star},\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}\tau_{\omega^{\prime}}/2+\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},T}^{2}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)\cdot n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{-\frac{2}{\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},\circ}}}

(S28)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tau_{\omega}}{2}+\frac{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\kappa_{\omega,T}^{2}}{2}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\cdot n_{\omega}^{-\frac{2}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}},

where in the last inequality we used that for
\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega
we have
\tau_{\omega^{\prime}}\leq\tau_{\omega}
,
\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},\circ}\leq\kappa_{\omega,\circ}
and
n_{\omega^{\prime}}\geq n_{\omega}
, which also implies that

\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\frac{\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)}{n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{2/\kappa_{\omega^{\prime},\circ}}}\leq\max_{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\omega^{\prime}\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}}}{{\omega^{\prime}\preceq\omega}}}\frac{\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega^{\prime}}/\delta)}{n_{\omega^{\prime}}^{2/\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\leq\frac{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{2}\cdot\frac{\log(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)}{n_{\omega}^{2/\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}.

Finally using that, for
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
,

n_{\omega}\geq\Bigl(16\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\kappa_{\omega,T}^{2}\cdot\log_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta)\Bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}},

we see that
\hat{\sigma}_{\omega}^{2}\leq\tau_{\omega}
. This completes the proof.
∎

Set

\delta=\delta_{n}:=\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}=\Bigl(\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}^{\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{-1}\geq\bigl(\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}\bigr)^{-1},

since
\alpha\beta_{\omega}\leq d_{\omega}
, for
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
. Let

C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}:=\Bigl(\frac{2^{7}C_{B}^{2}C_{\mathrm{L}}}{c_{\mathrm{E}}}\Bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}(1\vee\frac{1}{2\alpha})}\Bigl(\frac{20C_{\mathrm{S}}\kappa_{\omega,\mathrm{T}}\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{C_{\rho}^{1\vee\gamma_{\omega}}}\Bigr)^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}

for
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
and
C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}:=(16\kappa_{\omega,\circ}\kappa_{\omega,T}^{2}\bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}
for
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})
.

Assume initially that, for
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, we have
n_{\omega}\geq n_{\omega,\circ}
, where

n_{\omega,\circ}:=\max\biggl\{2^{d+1},C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}^{2}\cdot(8/e)^{4\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\bigl(\kappa_{\omega,\circ}+1+\alpha\bigr)^{4\kappa_{\omega,\circ}},\frac{1}{4}e^{\max_{\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})}\{C_{N,\omega}^{1/\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}(2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}/e+1)\}}\biggr\}.

(S29)

We then claim that the sample size conditions in Proposition
6

are satisfied. To see this, first fix
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
and note that by Lemma
S35
applied with
m=n_{\omega}
and
\epsilon=1/(4\kappa_{\omega,\circ})
we have

\log^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}(n_{\omega})\leq\Bigl(\frac{4\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}\Bigr)^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\cdot n_{\omega}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Therefore, using the first and second terms in the maximum in (
S29
), we have

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\log^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}_{+}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta_{n})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\log^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\Bigl(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}^{1+\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle=C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\biggl\{\log(2|\mathcal{N}|)+\Bigl(1+\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\Bigr)\cdot\log(n_{\omega})\biggr\}^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\Bigl(2+\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\Bigr)^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\cdot\Bigl(\frac{4\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}\Bigr)^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\cdot n_{\omega}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq n_{\omega},

for all
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
. Now, for
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
, again by Lemma
S35
applied with
m=n_{\omega}
, but
\epsilon=1/(2\kappa_{\omega,\circ})
, we have

\log(n_{\omega})\leq\Bigl(\frac{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}\Bigr)\cdot n_{\omega}^{\frac{1}{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}.

Then, for
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
, using the condition on
n_{\omega^{\prime}}
in the statement and the third term in (
S29
), we have

n_{\omega}\geq\log_{+}^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}\bigl(2|\mathcal{N}|\min_{\omega^{\prime}\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega^{\prime}}\bigr)\geq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}^{2}\cdot\Bigl(\frac{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}+1\Bigr)^{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}.

Therefore

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\log_{+}^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}(2|\mathcal{N}|n_{\omega}/\delta_{n})

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\Bigr(\log n_{\omega}+\log\bigl(2|\mathcal{N}|\min_{\omega^{\prime}\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega^{\prime}}\bigr)\Bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\Bigr(\frac{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}\cdot n_{\omega}^{\frac{1}{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}+n_{\omega}^{\frac{1}{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\Bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{N},\omega}\cdot\Bigr(\frac{2\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}{e}+1\Bigr)^{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}n_{\omega}^{1/2}\leq n_{\omega}

for all
\omega\in U(\Omega_{\star})\cap\mathcal{N}
.
It follows that, by the upper bound in Theorem
1

and Proposition
6

, if
n_{\omega}\geq n_{\omega,\circ}
for
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\Bigl\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\mathrm{HAM}})\Bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\leq\mathbb{E}_{Q}\bigl\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\Omega_{\star}})\bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\bigr\}+\mathbb{P}_{Q}\bigl(\hat{\Omega}\neq\Omega_{\star}\bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\bigr)

\displaystyle\hskip 30.0pt\leq C\cdot\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\Bigl(\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}\Bigr)\cdot\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{\beta_{\omega}\gamma_{\omega}(1+\alpha)}{\gamma_{\omega}(2\beta_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+\alpha\beta_{\omega}}}+4\delta_{n},

where
C:=1+2K_{\Omega_{\star}}\cdot\Bigl(\frac{3(1+\alpha)}{2}\Bigr)^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}\log_{+}^{\frac{1+\alpha}{2}}(2^{d+1})
.

Finally, if
n_{\omega}<n_{\omega,\circ}
for some
\omega\in\Omega_{\star}
, then we have that

\displaystyle\delta_{n}

\displaystyle=\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega}^{-\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\geq\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}\Bigl\{\bigl(\frac{n_{\omega,\circ}}{n_{\omega}}\bigr)^{\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\Bigr\}\cdot\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}n_{\omega,\circ}^{-\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\geq\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}\Bigl\{n_{\omega,\circ}^{-\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\Bigr\}.

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{Q}\Bigl\{\mathcal{E}_{P}(\hat{C}_{\mathrm{HAM}})\Bigm|O_{1}=o_{1},\ldots,O_{n}=o_{n}\Bigr\}\leq 1\leq\frac{\delta_{n}}{\min_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}\Bigl\{n_{\omega,\circ}^{-\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\Bigr\}}.

We therefore conclude that the overall result holds with

C_{\mathrm{U}}:=\max\biggl\{C+4,\max_{\omega\in\Omega_{\star}}\Bigl(n_{\omega,\circ}^{\frac{2(1+\alpha)}{\kappa_{\omega,\circ}}}\Bigr)\biggr\}.

∎

S4
Additional numerical results

S4.1
Cross-validation version of the HAM algorithm

If the parameters
\alpha
,
\bm{\beta}
, and
\bm{\gamma}
are unknown, we propose to use a cross-validation approach to estimate these. The empirical performance of this approach is illustrated in the numerical simulations in Sections
4

and
S4.2
. Our proposed procedure requires the user to choose a set of possible values for these parameters and employs
5
-fold cross-validation. For each of the
5
folds and each combination of parameter choices, the method calculates the empirical test error on the left out fold using the other
4
folds as training data. It returns whichever combination of parameter values minimises the empirical test error averaged over all folds, which can then be used in the HAM algorithm. The cross-validation procedure is given in Algorithm
S1
. The practitioner is left with choosing the range of possible values for the parameters. In our experiments, we have taken
A=\{0,1/2,1,d\}
as possible values for
\alpha
,
B=\{1/4,2/4,3/4,1\}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{d}
as candidate values for
\bm{\beta}
, and
G=\{1/2,1,2,\infty\}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{d}
as candidate values for
\bm{\gamma}
, and we recommend these as suitable default values.

Algorithm S1

A cross-validation procedure to choose
\alpha
,
\bm{\beta}
and
\bm{\gamma}
.

1:

Input
: Data
D_{n}=((X_{1}^{o_{1}},Y_{1},o_{1}),\ldots,(X_{n}^{o_{n}},Y_{n},o_{n}))\in(\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\{0,1\}\times\{0,1\}^{d})^{n}
, and finite sets
A\subseteq[0,\infty)
,
B\subseteq(0,1]^{d}
and
G\subseteq[0,\infty)^{d}
.

2:

Split
D_{n}
into
5
equally sized subsets uniformly at random, denote these
D_{n}^{(1)}
, …,
D_{n}^{(5)}
.

3:

for

(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})\in A\times B\times G

do

4:

E_{(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})}=0

5:

for

l\in[5]

do

6:

\hat{f}_{\mathbf{0}}(\cdot):=\frac{1}{|D_{n}|-|D_{n}^{(l)}|}\sum_{i\in[n]:(X_{i}^{o_{i}},Y_{i},o_{i})\notin D_{n}^{(l)}}Y_{i}-\frac{1}{2}

7:

for

(x^{o},y,o)\in D_{n}^{(l)}

do

8:

x:=x^{o}

9:

for

d^{\prime}=1,\ldots,d_{o}

do

10:

for

\omega\preceq o
such that
d_{\omega}=d^{\prime}

do

11:

b_{\omega}:=\min\{b_{j}:\omega_{j}=1\}
,
g_{\omega}:=\min\{g_{j}:\omega_{j}=1\}

12:

m_{\omega}:=\sum_{i\in[n]:(X_{i}^{o_{i}},Y_{i},o_{i})\notin D_{n}^{(l)}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{\omega\preceq o_{i}\}}

13:

k_{\omega}:=1+\lfloor m_{\omega}^{\frac{2b_{\omega}g_{\omega}}{g_{\omega}(2b_{\omega}+d_{\omega})+ab_{\omega}}}\rfloor
;

M_{\omega}:=\{i\in[n]:\omega\preceq o_{i},(X_{i}^{o_{i}},Y_{i},o_{i})\notin D_{n}^{(l)}\}

14:

Let
(X_{(1)_{\omega}}^{\omega}(x),Y_{(1)_{\omega}}(x)),\ldots,(X_{(m_{\omega})_{\omega}}^{\omega}(x),Y_{(m_{\omega})_{\omega}}(x))
be a reordering of the pairs
\{(X_{i}^{o_{i}},Y_{i}):i\in M_{\omega},(X_{i}^{o_{i}},Y_{i},O_{i})\notin D_{n}^{(l)}\}
such that
\|X_{(1)_{\omega}}^{\omega}(x)-x^{\omega}\|\leq\cdots\leq\|X_{(m_{\omega})_{\omega}}^{\omega}(x)-x^{\omega}\|

15:

\hat{f}_{\omega}(x):=\frac{1}{k_{\omega}}\sum_{j=1}^{k_{\omega}}Y_{(j)_{\omega}}(x)-\frac{1}{2}-\sum_{\omega^{\prime}\prec\omega}\hat{f}_{\omega^{\prime}}(x)

16:

end

for

17:

end

for

18:

\hat{\eta}(x):=\frac{1}{2}+\sum_{\omega\preceq o}\hat{f}_{\omega}(x)

19:

\hat{Y}:=\mathbbm{1}_{\{\hat{\eta}(x)\geq 1/2\}}

20:

E_{(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})}\leftarrow E_{(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{\hat{Y}\neq y\}}

21:

end

for

22:

end

for

23:

end

for

24:

Output

(\hat{\alpha},\hat{\bm{\beta}},\hat{\bm{\gamma}}):=\argmin_{(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})\in A\times B\times G}\bigl\{E_{(a,\mathbf{b},\mathbf{g})}\bigr\}
.

S4.2
Additional numerical experiments

Here we further investigate the performance of the HAM classifier in our framework. We consider the setting where there is in fact no missing data. We carried out similar experiments to those in Section
4

, where in each case, in contrast to the earlier experiments, we take our training data set to be
n=1000
independent copies of the pair
(X,Y)\sim P\equiv P_{Q}
. Since there is no missing data, the Complete Case approach and all imputation methods are equivalent to directly applying the
k
-nearest neighbour (
k
nn) algorithm to the training data. The results are presented in Figure
S1
– we see that the HAM classifier outperforms a direct application of
k
nn in each setting.

Fig S1:
Box plots of the empirical percentage test errors for the Complete Case (CC) approach, our HAM classifier with known parameters (HAM), and our classifier with parameters estimated using cross-validation (cvHAM). For reference, we also include our Oracle HAM (Oracle) classifier, and the Bayes risk is shown as the red horizontal line. Here there is no missing data, so the imputation methods would be the same as the complete case classifier. We present the results for Setting 1 (left), Setting 2 (middle) and Setting 3 (right) when
n=1000
.

S5
Auxiliary results

Lemma S35
.

For all
m\geq 1
and any
\epsilon>0
,
\log m\leq(m^{\epsilon})/(e\cdot\epsilon)
.

Fix
\epsilon>0
. Let
A_{\epsilon}:=\sup_{m\geq 1}((\log m)/m^{\epsilon})
, then our claim is that
A_{\epsilon}=1/(e\cdot\epsilon)
. To see this, let
g(m)=\log((\log m)/m^{\epsilon})=\log\log(m)-\epsilon\cdot\log m
, which is maximised at
m=e^{1/\epsilon}
, and indeed

\frac{\log(e^{1/\epsilon})}{e^{(1/\epsilon)\cdot\epsilon}}=\frac{1}{e\cdot\epsilon}.

∎
